SELECT "p0"."PRODUCT_ID", "p0"."BADGE", "p0"."BADGE_COLOR", "p0"."CATEGORY_ID", "p0"."DESCRIPTION", "p0"."IMAGE_URL", "p0"."IS_DELETED", "p0"."MANUFACTURER", "p0"."MINIMUM_AGE", "p0"."NAME", "p0"."PRICE", "p0"."RATING", "p0"."REVIEWS", "p0"."SEARCH_NAME", "p0"."STOCK_QUANTITY", "c"."CATEGORY_ID", "c"."CATEGORY_NAME"
FROM (
    SELECT "p"."PRODUCT_ID", "p"."BADGE", "p"."BADGE_COLOR", "p"."CATEGORY_ID", "p"."DESCRIPTION", "p"."IMAGE_URL", "p"."IS_DELETED", "p"."MANUFACTURER", "p"."MINIMUM_AGE", "p"."NAME", "p"."PRICE", "p"."RATING", "p"."REVIEWS", "p"."SEARCH_NAME", "p"."STOCK_QUANTITY"
    FROM "C##ORACLEDB2026"."PRODUCT" "p"
    WHERE NOT ("p"."IS_DELETED" <> 0 )
    ORDER BY "p"."NAME"
    OFFSET :p_p ROWS FETCH NEXT :p_p1 ROWS ONLY
) "p0"
